import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Plus,
  X,
  Phone,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { ServiceBookingRecord } from '../../data/mockData';
import { SPA_PACKAGES, formatCurrency } from '../../data/mockData';
import { STAFF_MEMBERS, type WorkspaceUserRole } from '../../data/adminData';

interface AdminAppointmentsViewProps {
  bookings: ServiceBookingRecord[];
  userRole?: WorkspaceUserRole;
  onAddBooking: (booking: ServiceBookingRecord) => void;
  onUpdateBookingStatus: (id: string, status: ServiceBookingRecord['status']) => void;
  onAssignStaff: (id: string, staffName: string) => void;
  onNotify: (msg: string) => void;
}

const WEEK_DAYS = [
  { date: '2026-11-14', label: 'Thứ 7 · 14/11' },
  { date: '2026-11-15', label: 'CN · 15/11' },
  { date: '2026-11-16', label: 'Thứ 2 · 16/11' },
  { date: '2026-11-17', label: 'Thứ 3 · 17/11' },
  { date: '2026-11-18', label: 'Thứ 4 · 18/11' },
  { date: '2026-11-19', label: 'Thứ 5 · 19/11' },
  { date: '2026-11-20', label: 'Thứ 6 · 20/11' },
];

const TIME_SLOTS = ['09:00 Sáng', '10:30 Sáng', '14:00 Chiều', '16:00 Chiều'];

const BOOKING_STATUS_META: Record<
  ServiceBookingRecord['status'],
  { label: string; cardCls: string; pillCls: string }
> = {
  upcoming: {
    label: 'Đã xác nhận',
    cardCls: 'border-[#93c5fd] bg-[#f8fbff]',
    pillCls: 'bg-[#eff5ff] text-[#0667d0]',
  },
  completed: {
    label: 'Hoàn thành',
    cardCls: 'border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8fafc]',
    pillCls: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700',
  },
  cancelled: {
    label: 'Đã hủy',
    cardCls: 'border-rose-200 bg-rose-50/40',
    pillCls: 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700',
  },
};

export const AdminAppointmentsView: React.FC<AdminAppointmentsViewProps> = ({
  bookings,
  userRole = 'admin',
  onAddBooking,
  onUpdateBookingStatus,
  onAssignStaff,
  onNotify,
}) => {
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<'week' | 'list'>('week');
  const [staffFilter, setStaffFilter] = useState<string>('all');
  const [serviceFilter, setServiceFilter] = useState<string>('all');
  const [selectedBooking, setSelectedBooking] = useState<ServiceBookingRecord | null>(null);
  const [showCreateModal, setShowCreateModal] = useState(false);

  // Create Booking Form State
  const [newPetName, setNewPetName] = useState('');
  const [newCustomerName, setNewCustomerName] = useState('');
  const [newCustomerPhone, setNewCustomerPhone] = useState('');
  const [newServiceId, setNewServiceId] = useState(SPA_PACKAGES[0].id);
  const [newDate, setNewDate] = useState('2026-11-18');
  const [newTimeSlot, setNewTimeSlot] = useState(TIME_SLOTS[0]);
  const [newStaff, setNewStaff] = useState(STAFF_MEMBERS[0]);
  const [newNote, setNewNote] = useState('');

  const upcomingCount = useMemo(
    () => bookings.filter((b) => b.status === 'upcoming').length,
    [bookings]
  );
  const completedCount = useMemo(
    () => bookings.filter((b) => b.status === 'completed').length,
    [bookings]
  );

  const filteredBookings = useMemo(() => {
    return bookings.filter((b) => {
      const matchStaff = staffFilter === 'all' || (b.staffName || 'Linh Phạm') === staffFilter;
      const matchService = serviceFilter === 'all' || b.serviceName === serviceFilter;
      return matchStaff && matchService;
    });
  }, [bookings, staffFilter, serviceFilter]);

  const handleCreateSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!newPetName.trim() || !newCustomerName.trim() || !newCustomerPhone.trim()) {
      onNotify('Vui lòng điền tên thú cưng, tên khách hàng và số điện thoại.');
      return;
    }
    const pkg = SPA_PACKAGES.find((p) => p.id === newServiceId) || SPA_PACKAGES[0];
    const created: ServiceBookingRecord = {
      id: `BK${Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 899999)}`,
      serviceName: pkg.name,
      petName: newPetName.trim(),
      date: newDate,
      timeSlot: newTimeSlot,
      price: pkg.price,
      status: 'upcoming',
      customerName: newCustomerName.trim(),
      customerPhone: newCustomerPhone.trim(),
      staffName: newStaff,
      note: newNote.trim() || undefined,
    };
    onAddBooking(created);
    setShowCreateModal(false);
    setNewPetName('');
    setNewCustomerName('');
    setNewCustomerPhone('');
    setNewNote('');
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 w-full min-w-0">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-[22px] sm:text-[26px] font-extrabold text-[#17324d] tracking-[-0.025em]">
            Lịch hẹn Dịch vụ Chăm sóc
          </h1>
          <p className="text-[13px] text-[#52657a] mt-1 tabular-nums">
            <strong className="text-[#17324d]">{bookings.length}</strong> lịch tuần này (14/11 – 20/11) ·{' '}
            <strong className="text-[#c2410c]">{upcomingCount}</strong> sắp tới ·{' '}
            <strong className="text-emerald-700">{completedCount}</strong> hoàn thành ·{' '}
            <span>{STAFF_MEMBERS.length} chuyên viên</span>
          </p>
        </div>

        {userRole === 'admin' && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setShowCreateModal(true)}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-[#c2410c] hover:bg-[#a93608] text-white text-[13px] font-bold cursor-pointer transition-colors"
          >
            <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Tạo lịch hẹn mới</span>
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Toolbar */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white border border-[#dfe6f0] p-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="inline-flex rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8faff] p-1">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setViewMode('week')}
            className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-[12px] font-bold cursor-pointer transition-colors ${
              viewMode === 'week'
                ? 'bg-[#fff2e9] text-[#c2410c]'
                : 'text-[#52657a] hover:text-[#17324d]'
            }`}
          >
            Lịch theo tuần
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setViewMode('list')}
            className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-[12px] font-bold cursor-pointer transition-colors ${
              viewMode === 'list'
                ? 'bg-[#fff2e9] text-[#c2410c]'
                : 'text-[#52657a] hover:text-[#17324d]'
            }`}
          >
            Danh sách ({filteredBookings.length})
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
          <select
            value={staffFilter}
            aria-label="Lọc theo chuyên viên"
            onChange={(e) => setStaffFilter(e.target.value)}
            className="h-9 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-white text-[12px] font-semibold text-[#17324d] cursor-pointer"
          >
            <option value="all">Tất cả chuyên viên</option>
            {STAFF_MEMBERS.map((s) => (
              <option key={s} value={s}>
                NV: {s}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>

          <select
            value={serviceFilter}
            aria-label="Lọc theo gói dịch vụ"
            onChange={(e) => setServiceFilter(e.target.value)}
            className="h-9 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-white text-[12px] font-semibold text-[#17324d] cursor-pointer"
          >
            <option value="all">Tất cả gói dịch vụ</option>
            {SPA_PACKAGES.map((pkg) => (
              <option key={pkg.id} value={pkg.name}>
                {pkg.name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Calendar or List */}
      {viewMode === 'week' ? (
        <div className="w-full max-w-full rounded-2xl bg-white border border-[#dfe6f0] overflow-hidden">
          <div className="w-full overflow-x-auto">
            <div className="min-w-[920px]">
              {/* Day Headers */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-8 border-b border-[#eef2f7] bg-[#f8faff] text-[12px]">
                <div className="p-3 font-bold text-[#52657a] border-r border-[#eef2f7]">
                  Khung giờ
                </div>
                {WEEK_DAYS.map((day) => {
                  const dayCount = filteredBookings.filter((b) => b.date === day.date).length;
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={day.date}
                      className={`p-3 border-r last:border-r-0 border-[#eef2f7] ${
                        day.date === '2026-11-18' ? 'bg-[#fff7f0]' : ''
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="font-extrabold text-[#17324d]">{day.label}</div>
                      <div className="text-[11px] text-[#52657a]">{dayCount} lịch hẹn</div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>

              {/* Time Rows */}
              {TIME_SLOTS.map((slot) => (
                <div key={slot} className="grid grid-cols-8 border-b last:border-b-0 border-[#eef2f7] min-h-[120px]">
                  <div className="p-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-[#52657a] border-r border-[#eef2f7] bg-[#fcfdff]">
                    {slot}
                  </div>
                  {WEEK_DAYS.map((day) => {
                    const cellBookings = filteredBookings.filter(
                      (b) => b.date === day.date && b.timeSlot === slot
                    );
                    return (
                      <div
                        key={`${day.date}-${slot}`}
                        className="p-2 border-r last:border-r-0 border-[#eef2f7] space-y-2"
                      >
                        {cellBookings.map((bk) => {
                          const meta = BOOKING_STATUS_META[bk.status];
                          return (
                            <button
                              key={bk.id}
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => setSelectedBooking(bk)}
                              className={`w-full text-left p-2.5 rounded-xl border ${meta.cardCls} hover:shadow-sm transition cursor-pointer space-y-1`}
                            >
                              <div className="text-[10px] font-bold text-[#52657a]">
                                #{bk.id}
                              </div>
                              <div className="text-[12px] font-extrabold text-[#17324d] line-clamp-1">
                                {bk.petName}
                              </div>
                              <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#52657a] line-clamp-2">
                                {bk.serviceName}
                              </div>
                              <div className="text-[10px] text-[#52657a]">
                                NV: {bk.staffName || 'Linh Phạm'}
                              </div>
                              <span className={`inline-block px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold ${meta.pillCls}`}>
                                {meta.label}
                              </span>
                            </button>
                          );
                        })}
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="px-4 py-3 bg-[#f8faff] border-t border-[#eef2f7] flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-[11px] text-[#52657a]">
            <span>Giờ mở cửa: 08:00 – 21:00 · Bấm vào thẻ lịch hẹn để cập nhật trạng thái hoặc phân công chuyên viên.</span>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-[#eff5ff] text-[#0667d0] font-bold">
                Đã xác nhận
              </span>
              <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 font-bold">
                Hoàn thành
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          {filteredBookings.map((bk) => {
            const meta = BOOKING_STATUS_META[bk.status];
            return (
              <div
                key={bk.id}
                onClick={() => setSelectedBooking(bk)}
                className="rounded-2xl bg-white border border-[#dfe6f0] hover:border-[#0667d0] p-4 space-y-2.5 cursor-pointer transition"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <span className="text-[12px] font-extrabold text-[#0667d0]">#{bk.id}</span>
                  <span className={`px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold ${meta.pillCls}`}>
                    {meta.label}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-[15px] font-extrabold text-[#17324d]">{bk.petName}</div>
                <div className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-[#52657a]">{bk.serviceName}</div>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-[#eef2f7] text-[12px]">
                  <span className="text-[#52657a]">
                    {bk.date} · {bk.timeSlot} · NV: <strong>{bk.staffName || 'Linh Phạm'}</strong>
                  </span>
                  <strong className="text-[#0667d0] tabular-nums">{formatCurrency(bk.price)}</strong>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Detail / Update Booking Modal */}
      {selectedBooking && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/45 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-[480px] rounded-2xl bg-white border border-[#dfe6f0] shadow-[0_24px_60px_rgba(23,50,77,0.2)] overflow-hidden">
            <div className="px-6 py-4 border-b border-[#eef2f7] flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-[16px] font-extrabold text-[#17324d]">
                  Chi tiết lịch hẹn #{selectedBooking.id}
                </h2>
                <div className="text-[12px] text-[#52657a]">
                  {selectedBooking.date} · {selectedBooking.timeSlot}
                </div>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSelectedBooking(null)}
                aria-label="Đóng"
                className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg hover:bg-[#eff5ff] flex items-center justify-center text-[#52657a] cursor-pointer"
              >
                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="p-6 space-y-4 text-[13px]">
              <div className="rounded-xl bg-[#f8faff] border border-[#dfe6f0] p-4 space-y-1.5">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-extrabold text-[#17324d] text-[15px]">
                  <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0667d0]" />
                  <span>{selectedBooking.petName}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="font-semibold text-[#0667d0]">{selectedBooking.serviceName}</div>
                <div className="text-[12px] text-[#52657a] flex items-center gap-3 pt-1">
                  <span>Khách: {selectedBooking.customerName || 'Khách PetsFriend'}</span>
                  {selectedBooking.customerPhone && (
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 tabular-nums">
                      <Phone className="w-3 h-3" />
                      {selectedBooking.customerPhone}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                {selectedBooking.note && (
                  <div className="text-[12px] text-[#52657a] pt-1">
                    Ghi chú: <em>{selectedBooking.note}</em>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[12px] font-bold text-[#17324d] mb-1">
                    Chuyên viên phụ trách
                  </label>
                  {userRole === 'staff' ? (
                    <div className="h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8faff] text-[13px] font-semibold flex items-center text-[#17324d]">
                      {selectedBooking.staffName || STAFF_MEMBERS[0]}
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <select
                      value={selectedBooking.staffName || STAFF_MEMBERS[0]}
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        const staff = e.target.value;
                        onAssignStaff(selectedBooking.id, staff);
                        setSelectedBooking((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, staffName: staff } : null));
                      }}
                      className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-white text-[13px] font-semibold"
                    >
                      {STAFF_MEMBERS.map((s) => (
                        <option key={s} value={s}>
                          {s}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[12px] font-bold text-[#17324d] mb-1">
                    Trạng thái lịch hẹn
                  </label>
                  {userRole === 'staff' ? (
                    <div className="h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8faff] text-[13px] font-bold flex items-center text-[#17324d]">
                      {BOOKING_STATUS_META[selectedBooking.status].label}
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <select
                      value={selectedBooking.status}
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        const next = e.target.value as ServiceBookingRecord['status'];
                        onUpdateBookingStatus(selectedBooking.id, next);
                        setSelectedBooking((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, status: next } : null));
                      }}
                      className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-white text-[13px] font-semibold"
                    >
                      <option value="upcoming">Đã xác nhận</option>
                      <option value="completed">Hoàn thành</option>
                      <option value="cancelled">Đã hủy</option>
                    </select>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="px-6 py-4 bg-[#f8faff] border-t border-[#eef2f7] flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
              {selectedBooking.status === 'upcoming' && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    onUpdateBookingStatus(selectedBooking.id, 'completed');
                    setSelectedBooking((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, status: 'completed' } : null));
                  }}
                  className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-[13px] font-bold cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Xác nhận hoàn thành nhiệm vụ
                </button>
              )}
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSelectedBooking(null)}
                className="px-5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-[#0667d0] text-white text-[13px] font-bold cursor-pointer"
              >
                Xong
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Create Booking Modal */}
      {showCreateModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/45 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <form
            onSubmit={handleCreateSubmit}
            className="w-full max-w-[500px] rounded-2xl bg-white border border-[#dfe6f0] shadow-[0_24px_60px_rgba(23,50,77,0.2)] overflow-hidden"
          >
            <div className="px-6 py-4 border-b border-[#eef2f7] flex items-center justify-between">
              <h2 className="text-[17px] font-extrabold text-[#17324d]">
                Tạo lịch hẹn dịch vụ mới
              </h2>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowCreateModal(false)}
                aria-label="Đóng"
                className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg hover:bg-[#eff5ff] flex items-center justify-center text-[#52657a] cursor-pointer"
              >
                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="p-6 space-y-4">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[12px] font-bold text-[#17324d] mb-1">
                    Tên thú cưng &amp; giống
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    placeholder="VD: Mít (Corgi 1 tuổi)"
                    value={newPetName}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewPetName(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8faff] text-[13px]"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[12px] font-bold text-[#17324d] mb-1">
                    Gói dịch vụ chăm sóc
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={newServiceId}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewServiceId(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8faff] text-[13px]"
                  >
                    {SPA_PACKAGES.map((pkg) => (
                      <option key={pkg.id} value={pkg.id}>
                        {pkg.name} ({pkg.priceStr})
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[12px] font-bold text-[#17324d] mb-1">
                    Tên chủ nuôi
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={newCustomerName}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewCustomerName(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8faff] text-[13px]"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[12px] font-bold text-[#17324d] mb-1">
                    Số điện thoại
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="tel"
                    required
                    value={newCustomerPhone}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewCustomerPhone(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8faff] text-[13px]"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[12px] font-bold text-[#17324d] mb-1">Ngày hẹn</label>
                  <select
                    value={newDate}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewDate(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8faff] text-[13px]"
                  >
                    {WEEK_DAYS.map((d) => (
                      <option key={d.date} value={d.date}>
                        {d.label}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[12px] font-bold text-[#17324d] mb-1">Khung giờ</label>
                  <select
                    value={newTimeSlot}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewTimeSlot(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8faff] text-[13px]"
                  >
                    {TIME_SLOTS.map((t) => (
                      <option key={t} value={t}>
                        {t}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[12px] font-bold text-[#17324d] mb-1">Chuyên viên</label>
                  <select
                    value={newStaff}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewStaff(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8faff] text-[13px]"
                  >
                    {STAFF_MEMBERS.map((s) => (
                      <option key={s} value={s}>
                        {s}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-[12px] font-bold text-[#17324d] mb-1">
                  Ghi chú tình trạng thú cưng
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="Lưu ý dị ứng, thói quen..."
                  value={newNote}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewNote(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8faff] text-[13px]"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="px-6 py-4 bg-[#f8faff] border-t border-[#eef2f7] flex justify-end gap-2.5">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowCreateModal(false)}
                className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-white text-[13px] font-bold text-[#17324d] cursor-pointer"
              >
                Hủy
              </button>
              <button
                type="submit"
                className="px-5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-[#c2410c] text-white text-[13px] font-bold cursor-pointer"
              >
                Xác nhận đặt lịch
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useCallback, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { flexRender, type RowSelectionState, type SortingState } from '@tanstack/react-table';
import {
  useLegacyTable as useReactTable,
  getCoreRowModel,
  getSortedRowModel,
  getPaginationRowModel,
  legacyCreateColumnHelper as createColumnHelper,
  type LegacyColumnDef,
} from '@tanstack/react-table/legacy';
import {
  Download,
  Plus,
  Search,
  ArrowUpDown,
  Pencil,
  Trash2,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { ProductCategory, ProductItem } from '../../data/mockData';
import { formatCurrency } from '../../data/mockData';
import {
  ADMIN_CATEGORY_LABELS,
  downloadCsvFile,
  type InventoryMode,
} from '../../data/adminData';
import type { ConfirmDialogState } from '../ConfirmDialog';
import { AdminPagination } from './AdminUiPrimitives';

interface AdminInventoryViewProps {
  products: ProductItem[];
  mode?: InventoryMode;
  readOnly?: boolean;
  onSaveProduct: (item: ProductItem, isNew: boolean) => void;
  onDeleteProduct: (id: string) => void;
  onRequestConfirm: (dialog: ConfirmDialogState) => void;
  onNotify: (msg: string) => void;
}

const columnHelper = createColumnHelper<ProductItem>();

const SUBTYPE_OPTIONS_BY_CAT: Record<Exclude<ProductCategory, 'spa'>, string[]> = {
  food: ['Dinh dưỡng & Hạt khô', 'Pate & Sốt thưởng'],
  hygiene: ['Thuốc & Chăm sóc da', 'Sữa tắm & Vệ sinh'],
  bed: ['Giường - Nệm - Thảm', 'Lồng - Chuồng'],
  backpack: ['Địu - Balo - Túi xách', 'Dây dắt - Vòng cổ', 'Hộp cát & Khay vệ sinh', 'Dụng cụ ăn uống'],
};

export const AdminInventoryView: React.FC<AdminInventoryViewProps> = ({
  products,
  mode = 'catalog',
  readOnly = false,
  onSaveProduct,
  onDeleteProduct,
  onRequestConfirm,
  onNotify,
}) => {
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const [categoryFilter, setCategoryFilter] = useState<'all' | Exclude<ProductCategory, 'spa'>>('all');
  const [stockFilter, setStockFilter] = useState<'all' | 'low' | 'in-stock' | 'promo'>(
    mode === 'stock' ? 'low' : 'all'
  );
  const [sorting, setSorting] = useState<SortingState>([]);
  const [rowSelection, setRowSelection] = useState<RowSelectionState>({});
  const [editingProduct, setEditingProduct] = useState<ProductItem | null>(null);
  const [isCreating, setIsCreating] = useState(false);

  // Form states for add/edit modal
  const [formName, setFormName] = useState('');
  const [formBrand, setFormBrand] = useState('PETSFRIEND SELECT');
  const [formCat, setFormCat] = useState<Exclude<ProductCategory, 'spa'>>('food');
  const [formSubType, setFormSubType] = useState('Dinh dưỡng & Hạt khô');
  const [formPetType, setFormPetType] = useState<'dog' | 'cat' | 'both'>('both');
  const [formPrice, setFormPrice] = useState(250000);
  const [formOriginalPrice, setFormOriginalPrice] = useState('');
  const [formStock, setFormStock] = useState(25);
  const [formImage, setFormImage] = useState('/images/prod-ceramic-bowl.jpg');
  const [formPackSize, setFormPackSize] = useState('Chó & Mèo • Tiêu chuẩn');
  const [formFeatured, setFormFeatured] = useState(false);

  const lowStockCount = useMemo(
    () => products.filter((p) => p.stockCount <= 15).length,
    [products]
  );

  const promoCount = useMemo(
    () => products.filter((p) => Boolean(p.discountBadge || p.originalPrice)).length,
    [products]
  );

  const filteredProducts = useMemo(() => {
    const q = searchTerm.trim().toLowerCase();
    return products.filter((item) => {
      const matchSearch =
        !q ||
        item.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        item.id.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        item.brand.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      const matchCat = categoryFilter === 'all' || item.category === categoryFilter;
      const matchStock =
        stockFilter === 'all' ||
        (stockFilter === 'low' && item.stockCount <= 15) ||
        (stockFilter === 'in-stock' && item.stockCount > 15) ||
        (stockFilter === 'promo' && Boolean(item.discountBadge || item.originalPrice));
      return matchSearch && matchCat && matchStock;
    });
  }, [products, searchTerm, categoryFilter, stockFilter]);

  const openModal = useCallback((item?: ProductItem) => {
    if (readOnly) return;
    if (item) {
      setIsCreating(false);
      setEditingProduct(item);
      setFormName(item.name);
      setFormBrand(item.brand);
      const safeCat: Exclude<ProductCategory, 'spa'> =
        item.category === 'spa' ? 'hygiene' : item.category;
      setFormCat(safeCat);
      setFormSubType(item.subType);
      setFormPetType(item.petType || 'both');
      setFormPrice(item.priceNum);
      setFormOriginalPrice(
        item.originalPrice ? item.originalPrice.replace(/\D/g, '') : ''
      );
      setFormStock(item.stockCount);
      setFormImage(item.image);
      setFormPackSize(item.packSize);
      setFormFeatured(Boolean(item.isFeatured));
    } else {
      setIsCreating(true);
      setEditingProduct(null);
      setFormName('');
      setFormBrand('PETSFRIEND SELECT');
      setFormCat('food');
      setFormSubType('Dinh dưỡng & Hạt khô');
      setFormPetType('both');
      setFormPrice(250000);
      setFormOriginalPrice('');
      setFormStock(25);
      setFormImage('/images/prod-ceramic-bowl.jpg');
      setFormPackSize('Chó & Mèo • Tiêu chuẩn');
      setFormFeatured(false);
    }
  }, [readOnly]);

  const handleSaveModal = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!formName.trim()) {
      onNotify('Vui lòng nhập tên sản phẩm.');
      return;
    }
    const origNum = Number(formOriginalPrice.replace(/\D/g, '')) || 0;
    const hasPromo = origNum > formPrice && formPrice > 0;
    const pct = hasPromo ? Math.round(((origNum - formPrice) / origNum) * 100) : 0;

    const base: ProductItem = editingProduct
      ? { ...editingProduct }
      : {
          id: `prod-custom-${Date.now().toString().slice(-5)}`,
          brand: formBrand.trim() || 'PETSFRIEND SELECT',
          name: formName.trim(),
          shortDesc: 'Sản phẩm chính hãng được phân phối trực tiếp tại PetsFriend Flagship.',
          description:
            'Sản phẩm chọn lọc chuẩn chất lượng PetsFriend, an toàn và phù hợp nhu cầu chăm sóc thú cưng hằng ngày.',
          packSize: formPackSize.trim() || 'Tiêu chuẩn',
          sizes: ['Tiêu chuẩn'],
          rating: 4.9,
          reviewCount: 1,
          priceNum: formPrice,
          price: formatCurrency(formPrice),
          badge: hasPromo ? `ƯU ĐÃI −${pct}%` : 'MỚI NHẬP',
          category: formCat,
          subType: formSubType as ProductItem['subType'],
          petType: formPetType,
          image: formImage.trim() || '/images/prod-ceramic-bowl.jpg',
          gallery: [formImage.trim() || '/images/prod-ceramic-bowl.jpg'],
          bgTone: '#eff5ff',
          stockCount: formStock,
          highlights: ['Chính hãng PetsFriend Flagship', 'Kiểm tra kỹ trước khi giao'],
          soldCount: 0,
          reviews: [],
        };

    const nextItem: ProductItem = {
      ...base,
      name: formName.trim(),
      brand: formBrand.trim().toUpperCase() || 'PETSFRIEND',
      category: formCat,
      subType: formSubType as ProductItem['subType'],
      petType: formPetType,
      priceNum: formPrice,
      price: formatCurrency(formPrice),
      originalPrice: hasPromo ? formatCurrency(origNum) : undefined,
      discountBadge: hasPromo ? `−${pct}%` : undefined,
      isFlashSale: hasPromo,
      badge: hasPromo ? `ƯU ĐÃI −${pct}%` : base.badge,
      stockCount: Math.max(0, formStock),
      image: formImage.trim() || base.image,
      packSize: formPackSize.trim() || base.packSize,
      isFeatured: !hasPromo && formFeatured,
    };

    onSaveProduct(nextItem, isCreating);
    setEditingProduct(null);
    setIsCreating(false);
  };

  const columns = useMemo(() => {
    const allCols = [
      columnHelper.display({
        id: 'select',
        header: ({ table }) => (
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            aria-label="Chọn tất cả sản phẩm trang này"
            checked={table.getIsAllPageRowsSelected()}
            onChange={table.getToggleAllPageRowsSelectedHandler()}
            className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#0667d0] rounded cursor-pointer"
          />
        ),
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            aria-label={`Chọn ${row.original.name}`}
            checked={row.getIsSelected()}
            onChange={row.getToggleSelectedHandler()}
            className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#0667d0] rounded cursor-pointer"
          />
        ),
      }),
      columnHelper.accessor('name', {
        header: 'Sản phẩm & SKU',
        cell: (info) => {
          const item = info.row.original;
          return (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-[240px]">
              <img
                src={item.image}
                alt={item.name}
                className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl object-cover border border-[#dfe6f0] shrink-0 bg-[#f8faff]"
              />
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="text-[13px] font-bold text-[#17324d] line-clamp-1">
                  {item.name}
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-[#52657a] mt-0.5">
                  <span className="font-mono uppercase">{item.id}</span>
                  <span>·</span>
                  <span>{item.brand}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        },
      }),
      columnHelper.accessor('category', {
        header: 'Danh mục',
        cell: (info) => {
          const cat = info.getValue();
          const label = cat === 'spa' ? 'Dịch vụ' : ADMIN_CATEGORY_LABELS[cat];
          return (
            <div>
              <div className="text-[12px] font-semibold text-[#17324d]">{label}</div>
              <div className="text-[11px] text-[#52657a]">{info.row.original.subType}</div>
            </div>
          );
        },
      }),
      columnHelper.accessor('stockCount', {
        header: 'Tồn kho',
        cell: (info) => {
          const count = info.getValue();
          return (
            <span
              className={`font-bold text-[13px] tabular-nums ${
                count <= 15 ? 'text-[#c2410c]' : 'text-[#17324d]'
              }`}
            >
              {count} sp
            </span>
          );
        },
      }),
      columnHelper.accessor('priceNum', {
        header: 'Giá bán',
        cell: (info) => {
          const item = info.row.original;
          return (
            <div className="tabular-nums">
              <div className="text-[13px] font-bold text-[#17324d]">
                {formatCurrency(info.getValue())}
              </div>
              {item.originalPrice && (
                <div className="text-[11px] text-[#52657a] line-through">
                  {item.originalPrice} ({item.discountBadge})
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        },
      }),
      columnHelper.display({
        id: 'status',
        header: 'Trạng thái',
        cell: ({ row }) => {
          const item = row.original;
          const isLow = item.stockCount <= 15;
          return (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
              <span
                className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold ${
                  isLow
                    ? 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700'
                    : 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700'
                }`}
              >
                {isLow ? 'Sắp hết hàng' : 'Còn hàng'}
              </span>
              {item.discountBadge && (
                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-[#fff2e9] text-[#c2410c] text-[10px] font-bold">
                  {item.discountBadge}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        },
      }),
      columnHelper.display({
        id: 'actions',
        header: 'Thao tác',
        cell: ({ row }) => {
          const item = row.original;
          return (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => openModal(item)}
                title="Chỉnh sửa sản phẩm"
                aria-label={`Chỉnh sửa ${item.name}`}
                className="p-2 rounded-lg border border-[#dfe6f0] hover:border-[#0667d0] hover:bg-[#eff5ff] text-[#17324d] hover:text-[#0667d0] cursor-pointer transition-colors"
              >
                <Pencil className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  onRequestConfirm({
                    title: 'Xác nhận xóa sản phẩm',
                    message: `Bạn có chắc chắn muốn xóa "${item.name}" khỏi kho PetsFriend?`,
                    confirmLabel: 'Xóa sản phẩm',
                    tone: 'danger',
                    onConfirm: () => onDeleteProduct(item.id),
                  })
                }
                title="Xóa sản phẩm"
                aria-label={`Xóa ${item.name}`}
                className="p-2 rounded-lg border border-[#dfe6f0] hover:border-rose-300 hover:bg-rose-50 text-[#52657a] hover:text-rose-700 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
              >
                <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              </button>
            </div>
          );
        },
      }),
    ];

    return (readOnly
      ? allCols.filter((c) => c.id !== 'select' && c.id !== 'actions')
      : allCols) as unknown as LegacyColumnDef<ProductItem>[];
  }, [onDeleteProduct, onRequestConfirm, openModal, readOnly]);

  const table = useReactTable({
    data: filteredProducts,
    columns,
    state: { sorting, rowSelection },
    onSortingChange: setSorting,
    onRowSelectionChange: setRowSelection,
    getCoreRowModel: getCoreRowModel(),
    getSortedRowModel: getSortedRowModel(),
    getPaginationRowModel: getPaginationRowModel(),
    initialState: { pagination: { pageIndex: 0, pageSize: 8 } },
  });

  const handleExportCsv = () => {
    downloadCsvFile(
      'pethub-inventory.csv',
      ['Mã SKU', 'Tên sản phẩm', 'Thương hiệu', 'Danh mục', 'Loại', 'Giá bán (VND)', 'Tồn kho', 'Khuyến mãi'],
      filteredProducts.map((p) => [
        p.id,
        p.name,
        p.brand,
        p.category === 'spa' ? 'Dịch vụ' : ADMIN_CATEGORY_LABELS[p.category],
        p.subType,
        p.priceNum,
        p.stockCount,
        p.discountBadge || 'Không',
      ])
    );
    onNotify(`Đã xuất ${filteredProducts.length} sản phẩm ra file CSV.`);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 w-full min-w-0">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-[22px] sm:text-[26px] font-extrabold text-[#17324d] tracking-[-0.025em]">
            {readOnly
              ? 'Tra cứu Sản phẩm & Tồn kho'
              : mode === 'stock'
                ? 'Kiểm soát Tồn kho & Cảnh báo'
                : 'Danh mục & Mặt hàng'}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-[13px] text-[#52657a] mt-1">
            {readOnly
              ? 'Chế độ Chỉ xem — Nhân viên tra cứu giá bán và tồn kho, không có quyền thêm/sửa/xóa sản phẩm.'
              : mode === 'stock'
                ? 'Theo dõi số lượng tồn kho, sản phẩm sắp hết (≤ 15) và cập nhật nhập hàng kịp thời.'
                : 'Quản lý danh mục sản phẩm, giá niêm yết và khuyến mãi đồng bộ với Storefront.'}
          </p>
        </div>

        {!readOnly ? (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleExportCsv}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-white hover:bg-[#f8faff] text-[#17324d] text-[13px] font-bold cursor-pointer transition-colors"
            >
              <Download className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>Xuất CSV / Excel</span>
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => openModal()}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-[#0667d0] hover:bg-[#0558b5] text-white text-[13px] font-bold cursor-pointer transition-colors"
            >
              <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>Thêm sản phẩm</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <span className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8faff] text-[12px] font-bold text-[#52657a]">
            Chỉ xem sản phẩm (Staff)
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Main Table Card */}
      <div className="w-full max-w-full rounded-2xl bg-white border border-[#dfe6f0] overflow-hidden">
        {/* Search & Filters */}
        <div className="p-4 sm:p-5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[#eef2f7] bg-[#f8faff]">
          <div className="relative flex-1 min-w-[220px] max-w-[420px]">
            <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-[#52657a] absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
            <input
              type="text"
              value={searchTerm}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Tìm theo tên sản phẩm, mã SKU hoặc hãng..."
              className="w-full h-10 pl-10 pr-4 rounded-xl bg-white border border-[#dfe6f0] text-[13px] text-[#17324d] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0667d0]"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
            {mode === 'stock' ? (
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={stockFilter === 'low'}
                onClick={() => setStockFilter(stockFilter === 'low' ? 'all' : 'low')}
                className={`h-10 px-3.5 rounded-xl border text-[13px] font-semibold cursor-pointer transition-colors ${
                  stockFilter === 'low'
                    ? 'bg-[#fff2e9] border-[#fdba74] text-[#c2410c]'
                    : 'bg-white border-[#dfe6f0] text-[#52657a] hover:text-[#17324d]'
                }`}
              >
                Sắp hết hàng ≤ 15 ({lowStockCount})
              </button>
            ) : (
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={stockFilter === 'promo'}
                onClick={() => setStockFilter(stockFilter === 'promo' ? 'all' : 'promo')}
                className={`h-10 px-3.5 rounded-xl border text-[13px] font-semibold cursor-pointer transition-colors ${
                  stockFilter === 'promo'
                    ? 'bg-[#eff5ff] border-[#0667d0] text-[#0667d0]'
                    : 'bg-white border-[#dfe6f0] text-[#52657a] hover:text-[#17324d]'
                }`}
              >
                Chỉ hàng giảm giá ({promoCount})
              </button>
            )}

            <select
              value={categoryFilter}
              aria-label="Lọc theo danh mục"
              onChange={(e) => setCategoryFilter(e.target.value as typeof categoryFilter)}
              className="h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-white text-[13px] font-semibold text-[#17324d] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0667d0] cursor-pointer"
            >
              {(Object.keys(ADMIN_CATEGORY_LABELS) as Array<keyof typeof ADMIN_CATEGORY_LABELS>).map(
                (key) => (
                  <option key={key} value={key}>
                    {ADMIN_CATEGORY_LABELS[key]}
                  </option>
                )
              )}
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Data Table */}
        <div className="w-full overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
            <thead className="bg-[#f8faff] border-b border-[#eef2f7] text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-[#52657a]">
              {table.getHeaderGroups().map((hg) => (
                <tr key={hg.id}>
                  {hg.headers.map((header) => (
                    <th key={header.id} className="px-4 py-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                      {header.isPlaceholder ? null : header.column.getCanSort() ? (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={header.column.getToggleSortingHandler()}
                          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 hover:text-[#17324d] cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          {flexRender(header.column.columnDef.header, header.getContext())}
                          <ArrowUpDown className="w-3 h-3" />
                        </button>
                      ) : (
                        flexRender(header.column.columnDef.header, header.getContext())
                      )}
                    </th>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              ))}
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#eef2f7] text-[13px]">
              {table.getRowModel().rows.length === 0 ? (
                <tr>
                  <td colSpan={7} className="py-12 text-center text-[#52657a]">
                    Không tìm thấy sản phẩm nào khớp bộ lọc hiện tại.
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : (
                table.getRowModel().rows.map((row) => (
                  <tr key={row.id} className="hover:bg-[#f8faff] transition-colors">
                    {row.getVisibleCells().map((cell) => (
                      <td key={cell.id} className="px-4 py-3 align-middle">
                        {flexRender(cell.column.columnDef.cell, cell.getContext())}
                      </td>
                    ))}
                  </tr>
                ))
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        <AdminPagination
          pageIndex={table.getState().pagination.pageIndex}
          pageCount={table.getPageCount()}
          totalRows={filteredProducts.length}
          pageSize={table.getState().pagination.pageSize}
          itemLabel="sản phẩm"
          onPageChange={(idx) => table.setPageIndex(idx)}
          onPageSizeChange={(size) => table.setPageSize(size)}
        />
      </div>

      {/* Add/Edit Modal */}
      {(isCreating || editingProduct) && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/45 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <form
            onSubmit={handleSaveModal}
            className="w-full max-w-[560px] rounded-2xl bg-white border border-[#dfe6f0] shadow-[0_24px_60px_rgba(23,50,77,0.2)] overflow-hidden"
          >
            <div className="px-6 py-4 border-b border-[#eef2f7] flex items-center justify-between">
              <h2 className="text-[17px] font-extrabold text-[#17324d]">
                {isCreating ? 'Thêm sản phẩm mới' : 'Chỉnh sửa sản phẩm'}
              </h2>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setEditingProduct(null);
                  setIsCreating(false);
                }}
                aria-label="Đóng"
                className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg hover:bg-[#eff5ff] flex items-center justify-center text-[#52657a] cursor-pointer"
              >
                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="p-6 space-y-4 max-h-[72vh] overflow-y-auto">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[12px] font-bold text-[#17324d] mb-1">
                  Tên sản phẩm
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={formName}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormName(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8faff] text-[13px] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0667d0]"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[12px] font-bold text-[#17324d] mb-1">
                    Danh mục
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={formCat}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const next = e.target.value as Exclude<ProductCategory, 'spa'>;
                      setFormCat(next);
                      setFormSubType(SUBTYPE_OPTIONS_BY_CAT[next][0]);
                    }}
                    className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8faff] text-[13px]"
                  >
                    <option value="food">Dinh dưỡng &amp; Pate</option>
                    <option value="hygiene">Thuốc &amp; Làm đẹp</option>
                    <option value="bed">Nệm &amp; Lồng chuồng</option>
                    <option value="backpack">Balo &amp; Phụ kiện</option>
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[12px] font-bold text-[#17324d] mb-1">
                    Loại chi tiết
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={formSubType}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormSubType(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8faff] text-[13px]"
                  >
                    {SUBTYPE_OPTIONS_BY_CAT[formCat].map((sub) => (
                      <option key={sub} value={sub}>
                        {sub}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[12px] font-bold text-[#17324d] mb-1">
                    Giá bán (₫)
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min={1000}
                    step={1000}
                    required
                    value={formPrice}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormPrice(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8faff] text-[13px] tabular-nums"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[12px] font-bold text-[#17324d] mb-1">
                    Giá gốc nếu giảm (₫)
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="Bỏ trống nếu giá thường"
                    value={formOriginalPrice}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormOriginalPrice(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8faff] text-[13px] tabular-nums"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[12px] font-bold text-[#17324d] mb-1">
                    Tồn kho
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min={0}
                    required
                    value={formStock}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormStock(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8faff] text-[13px] tabular-nums"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[12px] font-bold text-[#17324d] mb-1">
                    Dành cho thú cưng
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={formPetType}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormPetType(e.target.value as 'dog' | 'cat' | 'both')}
                    className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8faff] text-[13px]"
                  >
                    <option value="dog">Dành cho Chó</option>
                    <option value="cat">Dành cho Mèo</option>
                    <option value="both">Cả Chó &amp; Mèo</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[12px] font-bold text-[#17324d] mb-1">
                    Đường dẫn ảnh
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={formImage}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormImage(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-[#f8faff] text-[13px]"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <label className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-[13px] font-semibold text-[#17324d] cursor-pointer">
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={formFeatured}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormFeatured(e.target.checked)}
                  className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#0667d0] rounded"
                />
                <span>Gắn nhãn Sản phẩm nổi bật trên Trang chủ (áp dụng với giá thường)</span>
              </label>
            </div>

            <div className="px-6 py-4 bg-[#f8faff] border-t border-[#eef2f7] flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setEditingProduct(null);
                  setIsCreating(false);
                }}
                className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-white text-[13px] font-bold text-[#17324d] cursor-pointer"
              >
                Hủy
              </button>
              <button
                type="submit"
                className="px-5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-[#0667d0] hover:bg-[#0558b5] text-white text-[13px] font-bold cursor-pointer"
              >
                {isCreating ? 'Thêm vào kho' : 'Lưu thay đổi'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { flexRender } from '@tanstack/react-table';
import {
  useLegacyTable as useReactTable,
  getCoreRowModel,
  getPaginationRowModel,
  legacyCreateColumnHelper as createColumnHelper,
  type LegacyColumnDef,
} from '@tanstack/react-table/legacy';
import {
  Download,
  Search,
  Eye,
  Printer,
  X,
  Truck,
  Store,
  CheckCircle2,
  Landmark,
  Banknote,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { LookupOrderEntry, OrderStatus } from '../../data/mockData';
import { ORDER_STATUS_META, formatCurrency } from '../../data/mockData';
import {
  downloadCsvFile,
  isBankTransferPayment,
  type OrdersMode,
  type WorkspaceUserRole,
} from '../../data/adminData';
import type { ConfirmDialogState } from '../ConfirmDialog';
import { AdminPagination } from './AdminUiPrimitives';

interface AdminOrdersViewProps {
  orders: LookupOrderEntry[];
  mode?: OrdersMode;
  userRole?: WorkspaceUserRole;
  onUpdateOrderStatus: (orderId: string, nextStatus: OrderStatus) => void;
  onRequestConfirm: (dialog: ConfirmDialogState) => void;
  onNotify: (msg: string) => void;
}

const columnHelper = createColumnHelper<LookupOrderEntry>();

const parseOrderTimestampMs = (createdAt: string): number | null => {
  const dm = /(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})/.exec(createdAt);
  if (!dm) return null;
  const tm = /(\d{2}):(\d{2})/.exec(createdAt);
  const year = Number(dm[3]);
  const month = Number(dm[2]) - 1;
  const day = Number(dm[1]);
  const hour = tm ? Number(tm[1]) : 12;
  const minute = tm ? Number(tm[2]) : 0;
  return new Date(year, month, day, hour, minute, 0, 0).getTime();
};

export const AdminOrdersView: React.FC<AdminOrdersViewProps> = ({
  orders,
  mode = 'manage',
  userRole = 'admin',
  onUpdateOrderStatus,
  onRequestConfirm,
  onNotify,
}) => {
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<'all' | OrderStatus>('all');
  const [paymentFilter, setPaymentFilter] = useState<'all' | 'vnpay' | 'momo' | 'cod'>('all');
  const [staffFilter, setStaffFilter] = useState<string>('all');
  const [timeFrom, setTimeFrom] = useState<string>('');
  const [timeTo, setTimeTo] = useState<string>('');
  const [viewingOrder, setViewingOrder] = useState<LookupOrderEntry | null>(null);

  const staffOptions = useMemo(() => {
    const names = new Set<string>();
    for (const o of orders) {
      if (o.staffName) names.add(o.staffName);
    }
    return Array.from(names);
  }, [orders]);

  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    const completed = orders.filter((o) => o.status === 'completed');
    const pendingOrShipping = orders.filter(
      (o) => o.status === 'pending' || o.status === 'shipping'
    );
    const cancelled = orders.filter((o) => o.status === 'cancelled');
    return {
      total: orders.length,
      completedCount: completed.length,
      completedAmount: completed.reduce((s, o) => s + o.totalAmount, 0),
      pendingCount: pendingOrShipping.length,
      cancelledCount: cancelled.length,
    };
  }, [orders]);

  const filteredOrders = useMemo(() => {
    const q = searchTerm.trim().toLowerCase();
    const fromMs = timeFrom ? new Date(timeFrom).getTime() : null;
    const toMs = timeTo ? new Date(timeTo).getTime() : null;

    return orders.filter((o) => {
      const matchSearch =
        !q ||
        o.id.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        o.customerName.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        o.customerPhone.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (o.staffName || '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
      const matchStatus = mode === 'invoices' ? true : statusFilter === 'all' || o.status === statusFilter;
      const pmLower = o.paymentMethod.toLowerCase();
      const matchPayment =
        mode === 'manage'
          ? true
          : paymentFilter === 'all' ||
            (paymentFilter === 'vnpay' && pmLower.includes('vnpay')) ||
            (paymentFilter === 'momo' && pmLower.includes('momo')) ||
            (paymentFilter === 'cod' && (pmLower.includes('cod') || pmLower.includes('nhận hàng') || pmLower.includes('napas')));
      const matchStaff =
        mode === 'manage' ? true : staffFilter === 'all' || (o.staffName || 'Nhân viên trực') === staffFilter;

      let matchTime = true;
      if (mode === 'invoices' && (fromMs !== null || toMs !== null)) {
        const orderMs = parseOrderTimestampMs(o.createdAt);
        if (orderMs === null) {
          matchTime = false;
        } else {
          if (fromMs !== null && !Number.isNaN(fromMs) && orderMs < fromMs) matchTime = false;
          if (toMs !== null && !Number.isNaN(toMs) && orderMs > toMs) matchTime = false;
        }
      }

      return matchSearch && matchStatus && matchPayment && matchStaff && matchTime;
    });
  }, [orders, searchTerm, statusFilter, paymentFilter, staffFilter, timeFrom, timeTo, mode]);

  // Columns for "Quản lý đơn hàng": Mã đơn, Khách hàng, Sản phẩm & Giao nhận, Trạng thái (+ Xác nhận thành công), Xem chi tiết
  const manageColumns = useMemo(
    () => [
      columnHelper.accessor('id', {
        header: 'Mã đơn',
        cell: (info) => (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setViewingOrder(info.row.original)}
            className="text-[13px] font-extrabold text-[#0667d0] hover:underline cursor-pointer tabular-nums"
          >
            #{info.getValue()}
          </button>
        ),
      }),
      columnHelper.accessor('customerName', {
        header: 'Khách hàng',
        cell: (info) => {
          const o = info.row.original;
          return (
            <div className="min-w-[160px]">
              <div className="text-[13px] font-bold text-[#17324d]">{info.getValue()}</div>
              <div className="text-[11px] text-[#52657a] tabular-nums">{o.customerPhone}</div>
            </div>
          );
        },
      }),
      columnHelper.display({
        id: 'summary',
        header: 'Sản phẩm & Giao nhận',
        cell: ({ row }) => {
          const o = row.original;
          const first = o.items[0];
          const extra = o.items.length > 1 ? ` +${o.items.length - 1} sp` : '';
          return (
            <div className="min-w-[220px] max-w-[300px]">
              <div className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-[#17324d] truncate">
                {first ? `${first.name}${extra}` : 'Đơn hàng PetsFriend'}
              </div>
              <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-[#52657a] mt-0.5">
                {o.deliveryMethod === 'pickup' ? (
                  <Store className="w-3 h-3 text-[#0667d0]" />
                ) : (
                  <Truck className="w-3 h-3 text-[#52657a]" />
                )}
                <span className="truncate">
                  {o.deliveryMethod === 'pickup' ? 'Nhận tại cửa hàng Q.1' : 'Giao tận nơi'}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        },
      }),
      columnHelper.accessor('status', {
        header: 'Trạng thái đơn hàng',
        cell: (info) => {
          const row = info.row.original;
          const status = info.getValue();
          const canConfirmSuccess = status === 'pending' || status === 'shipping';

          return (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              {userRole === 'staff' ? (
                <span className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold ${ORDER_STATUS_META[status].badgeClass}`}>
                  {ORDER_STATUS_META[status].label}
                </span>
              ) : (
                <select
                  aria-label={`Trạng thái đơn ${row.id}`}
                  value={status}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const next = e.target.value as OrderStatus;
                    if (next === 'cancelled') {
                      onRequestConfirm({
                        title: 'Xác nhận hủy đơn hàng',
                        message: `Bạn có chắc muốn chuyển đơn #${row.id} sang trạng thái Đã hủy?`,
                        confirmLabel: 'Hủy đơn hàng',
                        tone: 'danger',
                        onConfirm: () => onUpdateOrderStatus(row.id, 'cancelled'),
                      });
                    } else {
                      onUpdateOrderStatus(row.id, next);
                    }
                  }}
                  className={`h-8 px-2.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold cursor-pointer focus:outline-none ${ORDER_STATUS_META[status].badgeClass}`}
                >
                  <option value="pending">Chờ xác nhận</option>
                  <option value="shipping">Đang giao hàng</option>
                  <option value="completed">Hoàn thành</option>
                  <option value="cancelled">Đã hủy</option>
                </select>
              )}

              {userRole === 'staff' && status === 'pending' && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onUpdateOrderStatus(row.id, 'shipping')}
                  className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-[#0667d0] hover:bg-[#0558b5] text-white text-[11px] font-bold cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                >
                  Xác nhận đơn

              {canConfirmSuccess && (

          <button

            title="Xem chi tiết đơn hàng"
            aria-label={`Xem chi tiết đơn hàng ${row.original.id}`}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-[#dfe6f0] hover:border-[#0667d0] hover:bg-[#eff5ff] text-[#0667d0] text-[12px] font-semibold cursor-pointer transition-colors whitespace-nowrap"
          >
            <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Xem chi tiết</span>

  // Columns for "Hóa đơn": Mã đơn hàng, Tên khách, Nhân viên bán (trực), Thời gian, Tổng tiền, Phương thức thanh toán
  const invoiceColumns = useMemo(
    () => [

      columnHelper.accessor('paymentMethod', {
        header: 'Phương thức thanh toán',
        cell: (info) => {
          const method = info.getValue();
          const isTransfer = isBankTransferPayment(method);
          return (

              className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold whitespace-nowrap ${

  const activeColumns = mode === 'invoices' ? invoiceColumns : manageColumns;

  const table = useReactTable({

                <strong className="text-[#0667d0]">{formatCurrency(stats.completedAmount)}</strong>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <strong className="text-[#17324d]">{stats.total}</strong> đơn ghi nhận ·{' '}
                <strong className="text-[#c2410c]">{stats.pendingCount}</strong> đang chờ/giao ·{' '}
                <strong className="text-emerald-700">{stats.completedCount}</strong> hoàn thành ·{' '}
                <span>{stats.cancelledCount} đã hủy</span>

            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-[#0667d0] hover:bg-[#0558b5] text-white text-[13px] font-bold cursor-pointer transition-colors"
          >
            <Download className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Xuất hóa đơn CSV</span>

        )}

            <input
              type="text"

                  : 'Tìm mã đơn (#PH...), tên khách hoặc SĐT...'
              }

            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
            {mode === 'manage' ? (
              <select
                value={statusFilter}

                onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value as typeof statusFilter)}
                className="h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-white text-[13px] font-semibold text-[#17324d] cursor-pointer"
              >
                <option value="all">Tất cả trạng thái</option>
                <option value="pending">Chờ xác nhận</option>
                <option value="shipping">Đang giao hàng</option>
                <option value="completed">Hoàn thành</option>
                <option value="cancelled">Đã hủy</option>
              </select>
            ) : (
              <>
                <select
                  value={staffFilter}
                  aria-label="Lọc nhân viên bán (trực)"
                  onChange={(e) => setStaffFilter(e.target.value)}
                  className="h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-white text-[13px] font-semibold text-[#17324d] cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <option value="all">Tất cả nhân viên</option>
                  {staffOptions.map((name) => (
                    <option key={name} value={name}>
                      {name}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>

                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
                  <input
                    type="datetime-local"
                    aria-label="Thời gian từ"
                    value={timeFrom}
                    onChange={(e) => setTimeFrom(e.target.value)}
                    className="h-10 px-2.5 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-white text-[12px] font-semibold text-[#17324d]"
                    title="Từ ngày giờ"
                  />
                  <span className="text-[12px] font-semibold text-[#52657a]">–</span>
                  <input
                    type="datetime-local"
                    aria-label="Thời gian đến"
                    value={timeTo}
                    onChange={(e) => setTimeTo(e.target.value)}
                    className="h-10 px-2.5 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-white text-[12px] font-semibold text-[#17324d]"
                    title="Đến ngày giờ"
                  />
                  {(timeFrom || timeTo) && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setTimeFrom('');
                        setTimeTo('');
                      }}
                      className="h-10 px-2.5 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-white hover:bg-[#eff5ff] text-[12px] font-bold text-[#0667d0] cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      Xóa lọc giờ
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>

                <select
                  value={paymentFilter}
                  aria-label="Lọc phương thức thanh toán"
                  onChange={(e) => setPaymentFilter(e.target.value as typeof paymentFilter)}
                  className="h-10 px-3 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-white text-[13px] font-semibold text-[#17324d] cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <option value="all">Mọi phương thức</option>
                  <option value="vnpay">VNPay</option>
                  <option value="momo">MoMo</option>
                  <option value="cod">COD / Napas</option>
                </select>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="w-full overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
            <thead className="bg-[#f8faff] border-b border-[#eef2f7] text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-[#52657a]">
              {table.getHeaderGroups().map((hg) => (
                <tr key={hg.id}>
                  {hg.headers.map((header) => (
                    <th key={header.id} className="px-4 py-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                      {flexRender(header.column.columnDef.header, header.getContext())}
                    </th>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              ))}
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#eef2f7]">
              {table.getRowModel().rows.length === 0 ? (
                <tr>
                  <td colSpan={activeColumns.length} className="py-12 text-center text-[13px] text-[#52657a]">
                    {mode === 'invoices'
                      ? 'Không có hóa đơn nào phù hợp bộ lọc.'
                      : 'Không có đơn hàng nào phù hợp bộ lọc.'}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : (
                table.getRowModel().rows.map((row) => (
                  <tr key={row.id} className="hover:bg-[#f8faff] transition-colors">
                    {row.getVisibleCells().map((cell) => (
                      <td key={cell.id} className="px-4 py-3.5 align-middle">
                        {flexRender(cell.column.columnDef.cell, cell.getContext())}
                      </td>
                    ))}
                  </tr>
                ))
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        <AdminPagination
          pageIndex={table.getState().pagination.pageIndex}
          pageCount={table.getPageCount()}
          totalRows={filteredOrders.length}
          pageSize={table.getState().pagination.pageSize}
          itemLabel={mode === 'invoices' ? 'hóa đơn' : 'đơn hàng'}
          onPageChange={(idx) => table.setPageIndex(idx)}
          onPageSizeChange={(size) => table.setPageSize(size)}
        />
      </div>

      {/* Order / Invoice Detail Modal */}
      {viewingOrder && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/45 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-[540px] rounded-2xl bg-white border border-[#dfe6f0] shadow-[0_24px_60px_rgba(23,50,77,0.2)] overflow-hidden">
            <div className="px-6 py-4 border-b border-[#eef2f7] flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-[17px] font-extrabold text-[#17324d]">
                  {mode === 'invoices'
                    ? `Chi tiết hóa đơn #${viewingOrder.id}`
                    : `Chi tiết đơn hàng #${viewingOrder.id}`}
                </h2>
                <div className="text-[12px] text-[#52657a]">{viewingOrder.createdAt}</div>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"

                <div>
                  <span className="text-[#52657a]">Khách hàng: </span>
                  <strong className="text-[#17324d]">{viewingOrder.customerName}</strong>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[#52657a]">SĐT: </span>
                  <strong className="text-[#17324d] tabular-nums">{viewingOrder.customerPhone}</strong>
                </div>

                  <strong className="text-[#17324d]">{viewingOrder.paymentMethod}</strong>

                  <strong className="text-[#0667d0]">
                    {viewingOrder.staffName || 'Nhân viên trực'}
                  </strong>
                </div>
                <div className="sm:col-span-2">
                  <span className="text-[#52657a]">Địa chỉ / Nhận hàng: </span>
                  <strong className="text-[#17324d]">{viewingOrder.shippingAddress}</strong>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="divide-y divide-[#eef2f7] border border-[#eef2f7] rounded-xl px-4">
                {viewingOrder.items.map((item) => (

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                      <img
                        src={item.image}
                        alt={item.name}
                        className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg object-cover border border-[#dfe6f0] shrink-0"
                      />
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <div className="text-[13px] font-bold text-[#17324d] truncate">
                          {item.name}
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-[11px] text-[#52657a]">
                          SL: {item.quantity} × {formatCurrency(item.price)}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-[13px] font-extrabold text-[#17324d] tabular-nums shrink-0">
                      {formatCurrency(item.price * item.quantity)}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>

                <span className="text-[13px] font-bold text-[#17324d]">Tổng thanh toán:</span>
                <span className="text-[19px] font-extrabold text-[#0667d0]">
                  {formatCurrency(viewingOrder.totalAmount)}
                </span>
              </div>

              <span className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold ${ORDER_STATUS_META[viewingOrder.status].badgeClass}`}>
                {ORDER_STATUS_META[viewingOrder.status].label}
              </span>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                {mode === 'manage' && userRole === 'staff' && viewingOrder.status === 'pending' && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      onUpdateOrderStatus(viewingOrder.id, 'shipping');
                      setViewingOrder((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, status: 'shipping' } : null));
                    }}
                    className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-[#0667d0] hover:bg-[#0558b5] text-white text-[13px] font-bold cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    Xác nhận đơn
                  </button>
                )}
                {mode === 'manage' &&
                  (viewingOrder.status === 'pending' || viewingOrder.status === 'shipping') && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        onUpdateOrderStatus(viewingOrder.id, 'completed');
                        setViewingOrder((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, status: 'completed' } : null));
                      }}
                      className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-[13px] font-bold cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      Xác nhận thành công
                    </button>
                  )}
                {mode === 'invoices' && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => window.print()}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-white text-[13px] font-bold text-[#17324d] cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <Printer className="w-4 h-4" />
                    <span>In hóa đơn</span>
                  </button>
                )}
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setViewingOrder(null)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-[#dfe6f0] bg-white text-[#17324d] text-[13px] font-bold cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Đóng
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
